{"parentUuid":"b0937a4d-85a9-411e-95a7-8454f7437f67","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8bbb47ca-a9ad-4f2d-9feb-815dcb6a893d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J97fKff92UR2AVCVTV5Df1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Tiny Startup Playbook\n     2→\n     3→Windows 10 22H2 PC with RTX 3060 Ti (8GB VRAM), running WSL2 Ubuntu for AI workloads.\n     4→\n     5→## Quick Reference\n     6→\n     7→| Service | URL/Port | Access |\n     8→|---------|----------|--------|\n     9→| SSH (WSL2) | 192.168.0.121:2222 | `ssh -p 2222 -i ~/.ssh/id_ed25519 root@192.168.0.121` |\n    10→| SSH (Windows) | 192.168.0.121:22 | `ssh -i ~/.ssh/id_ed25519 evie@192.168.0.121` |\n    11→| ComfyUI | http://192.168.0.121:8188 | Docker container in WSL2 |\n    12→| Ollama | http://192.168.0.121:11434 | Native in WSL2 |\n    13→| Docker TCP | 192.168.0.121:2375 | Remote Docker access |\n    14→\n    15→## Boot Sequence\n    16→\n    17→On Windows boot, the following happens automatically:\n    18→\n    19→1. **Windows SSH** starts (OpenSSH service)\n    20→2. **Scheduled Task \"WSL-PortProxy\"** runs `C:\\scripts\\wsl-portproxy.ps1` (as SYSTEM, at startup):\n    21→   - Waits for WSL to boot (up to 2 min, 10s retries)\n    22→   - Gets WSL2 internal IP via `wsl -d Ubuntu -- hostname -I`\n    23→   - Resets portproxy rules and creates new ones for ports 2222, 8188, 11434, 2375\n    24→   - Runs `/root/wsl-boot.sh` inside WSL to start sshd + dockerd (belt-and-suspenders with wsl.conf boot command)\n    25→   - Verifies sshd and dockerd are running\n    26→   - Logs to `C:\\scripts\\portproxy.log`\n    27→3. **WSL2 Ubuntu** boots on first `wsl` command, `/etc/wsl.conf` `[boot] command` runs `/root/wsl-boot.sh`\n    28→4. **systemd disabled** -- services started by `wsl-boot.sh` (uses `nohup` + `disown` to survive parent exit)\n    29→\n    30→### Service Start Scripts"}]},"uuid":"32aa5c62-e664-4408-97ac-527f16eb615b","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:49:43.349Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/docs/services/tiny-startup-playbook.md","content":"# Tiny Startup Playbook\n\nWindows 10 22H2 PC with RTX 3060 Ti (8GB VRAM), running WSL2 Ubuntu for AI workloads.\n\n## Quick Reference\n\n| Service | URL/Port | Access |\n|---------|----------|--------|\n| SSH (WSL2) | 192.168.0.121:2222 | `ssh -p 2222 -i ~/.ssh/id_ed25519 root@192.168.0.121` |\n| SSH (Windows) | 192.168.0.121:22 | `ssh -i ~/.ssh/id_ed25519 evie@192.168.0.121` |\n| ComfyUI | http://192.168.0.121:8188 | Docker container in WSL2 |\n| Ollama | http://192.168.0.121:11434 | Native in WSL2 |\n| Docker TCP | 192.168.0.121:2375 | Remote Docker access |\n\n## Boot Sequence\n\nOn Windows boot, the following happens automatically:\n\n1. **Windows SSH** starts (OpenSSH service)\n2. **Scheduled Task \"WSL-PortProxy\"** runs `C:\\scripts\\wsl-portproxy.ps1` (as SYSTEM, at startup):\n   - Waits for WSL to boot (up to 2 min, 10s retries)\n   - Gets WSL2 internal IP via `wsl -d Ubuntu -- hostname -I`\n   - Resets portproxy rules and creates new ones for ports 2222, 8188, 11434, 2375\n   - Runs `/root/wsl-boot.sh` inside WSL to start sshd + dockerd (belt-and-suspenders with wsl.conf boot command)\n   - Verifies sshd and dockerd are running\n   - Logs to `C:\\scripts\\portproxy.log`\n3. **WSL2 Ubuntu** boots on first `wsl` command, `/etc/wsl.conf` `[boot] command` runs `/root/wsl-boot.sh`\n4. **systemd disabled** -- services started by `wsl-boot.sh` (uses `nohup` + `disown` to survive parent exit)\n\n### Service Start Scripts","numLines":30,"startLine":1,"totalLines":219}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b0937a4d-85a9-411e-95a7-8454f7437f67","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

A vertical scrollable PNG (webtoon-style), split into chunks if >65000px tall\n     7→  2. A self-contained HTML viewer with navigation and table of contents\n     8→\n     9→Usage:\n    10→  python3 tools/assemble-panels.py                          # defaults for pilot-a\n    11→  python3 tools/assemble-panels.py --narration              # include narrative text\n    12→  python3 tools/assemble-panels.py --no-image               # HTML only (faster)\n    13→  python3 tools/assemble-panels.py --width 800              # narrower layout\n    14→\"\"\"\n    15→\n    16→import argparse\n    17→import json\n    18→import os\n    19→import re\n    20→import sys\n    21→from pathlib import Path\n    22→\n    23→try:\n    24→    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont\n    25→except ImportError:\n    26→    print(\"ERROR: Pillow is required. Install with: pip3 install Pillow\", file=sys.stderr)\n    27→    sys.exit(1)\n    28→\n    29→\n    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    31→# Defaults\n    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    33→BASE = Path(\"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov\")\n    34→DEFAULT_JOBS = BASE / \"production\" / \"panel-jobs.json\"\n    35→DEFAULT_PANELS = BASE / \"assets/pilots/pilot-a/panels\"\n    36→DEFAULT_OUTPUT = BASE / \"assets/pilots/pilot-a\"\n    37→\n    38→TARGET_WIDTH = 1200\n    39→PANEL_GAP = 24\n    40→TITLE_BAR_HEIGHT = 48\n    41→TEXT_BLOCK_PAD = 16\n    42→TEXT_LINE_HEIGHT = 22\n    43→MAX_IMAGE_HEIGHT = 65000  # PNG spec ~65535, leave margin\n    44→BG_COLOR = (18, 18, 22)\n    45→TITLE_BG = (30, 30, 38)\n    46→TITLE_FG = (220, 200, 160)\n    47→TEXT_FG = (200, 200, 200)\n    48→MISSING_BG = (50, 50, 55)\n    49→MISSING_FG = (140, 140, 140)\n    50→\n    51→\n    52→def parse_args():\n    53→    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Assemble panel images into a showable draft\")\n    54→    p.add_argument(\"--jobs\", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_JOBS,\n    55→                   help=\"Path to panel-jobs.json\")\n    56→    p.add_argument(\"--panels\", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_PANELS,\n    57→                   help=\"Directory containing panel images\")\n    58→    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_OUTPUT,\n    59→                   help=\"Output directory\")\n    60→    p.add_argument(\"--width\", type=int, default=TARGET_WIDTH,\n    61→                   help=\"Target width in pixels (default: 1200)\")\n    62→    p.add_argument(\"--no-image\", action=\"store_true\",\n    63→                   help=\"Skip PNG generation (HTML only)\")\n    64→    p.add_argument(\"--no-html\", action=\"store_true\",\n    65→                   help=\"Skip HTML generation (PNG only)\")\n    66→    p.add_argument(\"--narration\", action=\"store_true\",\n    67→                   help=\"Include narrative text below panels\")\n    68→    return p.parse_args()\n    69→\n    70→\n    71→def load_jobs(jobs_path: Path) -> list:\n    72→    with open(jobs_path) as f:\n    73→        return json.load(f)\n    74→\n    75→\n    76→def extract_title(filename: str) -> str:\n    77→    \"\"\"Extract human-readable title from filename.\n    78→\n    79→    panel-01-conquis-abbey.png -> '01 - Conquis Abbey'\n    80→    panel-31-a-vignette-quick-cuts-three-figures.png -> '31a - Vignette Quick Cuts ...'\n    81→    \"\"\"\n    82→    stem = Path(filename).stem\n    83→    stem = re.sub(r'^panel-', '', stem)\n    84→    stem = re.sub(r'-draft$', '', stem)\n    85→    m = re.match(r'^(\\d+)(?:-([a-d]))?-(.+)$', stem)\n    86→    if m:\n    87→        num, suffix, name = m.group(1), m.group(2) or '', m.group(3)\n    88→        name = name.replace('-', ' ').title()\n    89→        return f\"{num}{suffix} - {name}\"\n    90→    return stem.replace('-', ' ').title()\n    91→\n    92→\n    93→def extract_narration(prompt_original: str) -> str:\n    94→    \"\"\"Strip style/tone suffixes from the original prompt to get narrative text.\"\"\"\n    95→    if not prompt_original:\n    96→        return \"\"\n    97→    raw = prompt_original\n    98→    for marker in [\"Gothic anime style\", \"gothic anime style\"]:\n    99→        idx = raw.find(marker)\n   100→        if idx > 0:\n   101→            raw = raw[:idx].rstrip(\". ,\")\n   102→            break\n   103→    return raw\n   104→\n   105→\n   106→def find_image(job: dict, panels_dir: Path) -> \"Path | None\":\n   107→    \"\"\"Find the best image file for a panel job.\n   108→\n   109→    Priority: exact non-draft > polished > any non-draft > draft of canonical > any draft.\n   110→    Skips BEFORE/AFTER comparison images.\n   111→    \"\"\"\n   112→    canonical = job[\"filename\"]\n   113→    panel_num = job[\"panel\"]\n   114→    stem = Path(canonical).stem  # e.g. panel-01-conquis-abbey\n   115→\n   116→    # 1. Exact canonical file (non-draft, highest quality)\n   117→    exact = panels_dir / canonical\n   118→    if exact.exists():\n   119→        return exact\n   120→\n   121→    num_str = f\"{panel_num:02d}\" if isinstance(panel_num, int) else str(panel_num)\n   122→\n   123→    # Detect if this job has a letter suffix (e.g. panel-31-a-...)\n   124→    suffix_match = re.match(r'^panel-\\d+-([a-d])-.+\\.png$', canonical)\n   125→    letter = suffix_match.group(1) if suffix_match else None\n   126→\n   127→    # Collect all panel files matching this number\n   128→    if letter:\n   129→        # Match both \"panel-31-a-...\" and \"panel-31a-...\" naming conventions\n   130→        pattern = re.compile(\n   131→            rf'^panel-{re.escape(num_str)}-?{re.escape(letter)}-.+\\.png$',\n   132→            re.IGNORECASE,\n   133→        )\n   134→    else:\n   135→        # Match panel number but not letter-suffixed variants\n   136→        pattern = re.compile(\n   137→            rf'^panel-{re.escape(num_str)}-(?![a-d]-)(.+)\\.png$',\n   138→            re.IGNORECASE,\n   139→        )\n   140→\n   141→    candidates = [f for f in panels_dir.iterdir() if pattern.match(f.name)]\n   142→\n   143→    # Filter out BEFORE/AFTER comparison images\n   144→    candidates = [\n   145→        f for f in candidates\n   146→        if '-BEFORE' not in f.name and '-AFTER' not in f.name\n   147→    ]\n   148→\n   149→    non_draft = [f for f in candidates if '-draft' not in f.stem]\n   150→    draft = [f for f in candidates if '-draft' in f.stem]\n   151→\n   152→    # Prefer polished > any non-draft\n   153→    polished = [f for f in non_draft if 'polished' in f.stem.lower()]\n   154→    if polished:\n   155→        return polished[0]\n   156→    if non_draft:\n   157→        return non_draft[0]\n   158→\n   159→    # Draft version of the canonical name\n   160→    draft_canonical = panels_dir / (stem + \"-draft.png\")\n   161→    if draft_canonical.exists():\n   162→        return draft_canonical\n   163→\n   164→    # Any draft for this panel\n   165→    if draft:\n   166→        return draft[0]\n   167→\n   168→    return None\n   169→\n   170→\n   171→def get_font(size: int):\n   172→    \"\"\"Load a system font, falling back to Pillow default.\"\"\"\n   173→    for fp in [\n   174→        \"/System/Library/Fonts/Helvetica.ttc\",\n   175→        \"/System/Library/Fonts/SFNSMono.ttf\",\n   176→        \"/System/Library/Fonts/Menlo.ttc\",\n   177→        \"/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf\",\n   178→    ]:\n   179→        if os.path.exists(fp):\n   180→            try:\n   181→                return ImageFont.truetype(fp, size)\n   182→            except Exception:\n   183→                continue\n   184→    return ImageFont.load_default()\n   185→\n   186→\n   187→def wrap_text(text: str, font, max_width: int, draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw) -> list:\n   188→    \"\"\"Word-wrap text to fit within max_width pixels.\"\"\"\n   189→    words = text.split()\n   190→    lines = []\n   191→    current = \"\"\n   192→    for word in words:\n   193→        test = f\"{current} {word}\".strip()\n   194→        bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), test, font=font)\n   195→        if bbox[2] - bbox[0] <= max_width:\n   196→            current = test\n   197→        else:\n   198→            if current:\n   199→                lines.append(current)\n   200→            current = word\n   201→    if current:\n   202→        lines.append(current)\n   203→    return lines or [\"\"]\n   204→\n   205→\n   206→def render_panel_block(\n   207→    job: dict,\n   208→    panels_dir: Path,\n   209→    target_width: int,\n   210→    title_font,\n   211→    text_font,\n   212→    include_narration: bool,\n   213→) -> \"tuple[Image.Image | None, str, bool]\":\n   214→    \"\"\"Render a single panel block: title bar + image + optional narration.\n   215→\n   216→    Returns (composite_image, status_label, is_missing).\n   217→    \"\"\"\n   218→    title = extract_title(job[\"filename\"])\n   219→    img_path = find_image(job, panels_dir)\n   220→    is_missing = img_path is None\n   221→    is_draft = img_path is not None and \"-draft\" in img_path.name\n   222→\n   223→    narration = extract_narration(job.get(\"prompt_original\", \"\")) if include_narration else \"\"\n   224→\n   225→    # Scratch surface for text measurement\n   226→    scratch = Image.new(\"RGB\", (target_width, 100))\n   227→    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(scratch)\n   228→\n   229→    # Status label\n   230→    status = \"\"\n   231→    if is_missing:\n   232→        status = \" [MISSING]\"\n   233→    elif is_draft:\n   234→        status = \" [draft]\"\n   235→    title_text = f\"Panel {title}{status}\"\n   236→\n   237→    # Measure narration\n   238→    narration_lines = []\n   239→    narration_height = 0\n   240→    if narration:\n   241→        narration_lines = wrap_text(narration, text_font, target_width - TEXT_BLOCK_PAD * 2, draw)\n   242→        narration_height = len(narration_lines) * TEXT_LINE_HEIGHT + TEXT_BLOCK_PAD * 2\n   243→\n   244→    # Load and scale panel image, or create placeholder\n   245→    if img_path:\n   246→        try:\n   247→            panel_img = Image.open(img_path).convert(\"RGB\")\n   248→            scale = target_width / panel_img.width\n   249→            new_h = int(panel_img.height * scale)\n   250→            panel_img = panel_img.resize((target_width, new_h), Image.LANCZOS)\n   251→        except Exception as e:\n   252→            print(f\"  WARNING: Could not load {img_path.name}: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n   253→            img_path = None\n   254→            is_missing = True\n   255→\n   256→    if is_missing:\n   257→        placeholder_h = 400\n   258→        panel_img = Image.new(\"RGB\", (target_width,
